<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-660AD016-0BD4-49EF-94D6-8C0BF6ACD4D2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

There are $27$ students in Mrs Din's class.

Mrs Din buys $4$ boxes of chocolates.

There are $36$ chocolates in each box.

She shares out the chocolates equally amongst her $27$ students.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the number of chocolates each student receives.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of chocolates each student receives} &= \dfrac {\text {Total chocolates}} {\text {Total students}} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {36 \times 4} {27} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 4 \times 4} {9 \times 3} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times 4 \times 4} {\cancel {9} \times 3} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {16} {3} \\\\
                                                   &= 5 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                                   &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of chocolates each student receives} &= \dfrac {\text {Total chocolates}} {\text {Total students}} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {36 \times 4} {27} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 4 \times 4} {9 \times 3} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times 4 \times 4} {\cancel {9} \times 3} \\\\
                                                   &= \dfrac {16} {3} \\\\
                                                   &= 5 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                                   &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the number of chocolates left over.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of chocolates left over} &= 36 \times 4 - 27 \times 5 \\\\
                                       &= 144 - 135 \\\\
                                       &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$9$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of chocolates left over} &= 36 \times 4 - 27 \times 5 \\\\
                                       &= 144 - 135 \\\\
                                       &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C37C682E-26E5-4DA2-98A6-BB49C4AEA7A6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The number of insects in a colony doubles each week. If there were $100$ insects in the colony at the start of the first week,

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

how many would there be after $6$ weeks?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Inserts after six weeks} &= 2 ^ 6 \times 100 \\\\
                               &= 64 \times 100 \\\\
                               &= 6400
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Inserts after six weeks} &= 2 ^ 6 \times 100 \\\\
                               &= 64 \times 100 \\\\
                               &= 6400
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6400$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

After how many weeks would there first be more than $100000$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of weeks $=a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 ^ a \times 100 &= 100000 \\\\
2 ^ a            &= \dfrac {100000} {100} \\\\
2 ^ a            &= \dfrac {1000 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
2 ^ a            &= 1000
\end{aligned}
$

| a    |   Value of 2 ^ a |
|:----:|:----------------:|
| $9$  |       $512$      |
| $10$ |       $1024$     |

$a = 10$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of weeks $=a$
<emptyline>

2 ^ a            &= 1000
\end{aligned}
$

|:----:|:----------------:|
| $9$  |       $512$      |
| $10$ |       $1024$     |

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-632B3C63-276D-4B09-BFCB-80AB16809792'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The distance between Richmond and Kingston is approximately $3 \dfrac{3}{8} \ \text{miles}$. If I walk $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of the way, 
how far do I walk?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Distance travelled} &= \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text {of} \ 3 \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \dfrac {27} {8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 27} {3 \times 8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 9} {3 \times 2 \times 4} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times 9} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {2} \times 4} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {9} {4} \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {9} {4} \ \text {miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Distance travelled} &= \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text {of} \ 3 \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \dfrac {27} {8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 27} {3 \times 8} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 9} {3 \times 2 \times 4} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times 9} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {2} \times 4} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {9} {4} \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {9} {4} \ \text {miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-87808858-372B-4F74-AE78-99248501465B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A boy was asked to take $3.51$ away from $7.9$, but by mistake he took away $3.15$ instead. By how much is he wrong?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {He was wrong by} &= 3.51 - 3.15 \\\\
                        &= 0.36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {He was wrong by} &= 3.51 - 3.15 \\\\
                        &= 0.36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AA8ED085-BF3B-4E90-AC3B-C92BC01655FF'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A bag contains $49$ red balls and $1$ blue ball. How many red balls should be removed for the bag to contain $90\%$ red balls?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of red balls removed $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {49 - b} {50 - b}                                             &= \dfrac {90} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times (50 - b) \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 50 - \dfrac {90} {100} \times b \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {45 \times 2 \times 50} {50 \times 2} - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {45 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {50}} {\cancel {50} \times \cancel {2}} - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= 45 - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
-b + \dfrac {90 b} {100}                                             &= 45 - 49 \\\\
-\dfrac {b \times 100} {1 \times 100} + \dfrac {90 b} {100}          &= -4 \\\\
-\dfrac {100 b + 90 b} {100}                                         &= -4 \\\\
-\dfrac {10 b} {100}                                                 &= -4 \\\\
\cancel {-} \dfrac {1 \cancel {0} b} {10 \cancel {0}}                &= \cancel {-} 4 \\\\
\dfrac {b} {10}                                                      &= 4 \\\\
b                                                                    &= 4 \times 10 \\\\
b                                                                    &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Alternate solution.

Let the total number of balls after red balls removed $= a$

Number of blue balls,

$
\begin{aligned}
10 \% \ \text{of} \ a            &= 1 \\\\
\dfrac {10} {100} \times a   &= 1 \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {100} {10} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {10 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0} \\\\
a                            &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Red balls}           &= \text{Total balls} - \text {Blue balls} \\\\
                           &= 10 - 1 \\\\
                           &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Red balls removed}   &= 49 - 9 \\\\
                           &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of red balls removed $= b$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {49 - b} {50 - b}                                             &= \dfrac {90} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times (50 - b) \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 50 - \dfrac {90} {100} \times b \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {45 \times 2 \times 50} {50 \times 2} - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= \dfrac {45 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {50}} {\cancel {50} \times \cancel {2}} - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
49 - b                                                               &= 45 - \dfrac {90 b} {100} \\\\
-b + \dfrac {90 b} {100}                                             &= 45 - 49 \\\\
-\dfrac {b \times 100} {1 \times 100} + \dfrac {90 b} {100}          &= -4 \\\\
-\dfrac {100 b + 90 b} {100}                                         &= -4 \\\\
-\dfrac {10 b} {100}                                                 &= -4 \\\\
\cancel {-} \dfrac {1 \cancel {0} b} {10 \cancel {0}}                &= \cancel {-} 4 \\\\
\dfrac {b} {10}                                                      &= 4 \\\\
b                                                                    &= 4 \times 10 \\\\
b                                                                    &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Alternate solution.
<emptyline>
Let the total number of balls after red balls removed $= a$
<emptyline>
Number of blue balls,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
10 \% \ \text{of} \ a            &= 1 \\\\
\dfrac {10} {100} \times a   &= 1 \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {100} {10} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {10 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0} \\\\
a                            &= 10
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Red balls}           &= \text{Total balls} - \text {Blue balls} \\\\
                           &= 10 - 1 \\\\
                           &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Red balls removed}   &= 49 - 9 \\\\
                           &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-16D089A8-F897-466C-A647-02A29D9F4503'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

If a basketball weighs $300 \ \text{g}$ plus half its own weight, how much does it weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the weight of basketball $= b$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Weight of the basketball}                  &= 300 + \dfrac {1} {2} \times b \\\\
b                                                 &= 300 + \dfrac {b} {2} \\\\
b - \dfrac {b} {2}                                &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {b \times 2} {1 \times 2} - \dfrac {b} {2} &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {2 b} {2} - \dfrac {b} {2}                 &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {2 b - b} {2}                              &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {b} {2}                                    &= 300 \\\\
b                                                 &= 300 \times 2 \\\\
b                                                 &= 600 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$600 \ \text{g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the weight of basketball $= b$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Weight of the basketball}                  &= 300 + \dfrac {1} {2} \times b \\\\
b                                                 &= 300 + \dfrac {b} {2} \\\\
b - \dfrac {b} {2}                                &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {b \times 2} {1 \times 2} - \dfrac {b} {2} &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {2 b} {2} - \dfrac {b} {2}                 &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {2 b - b} {2}                              &= 300 \\\\
\dfrac {b} {2}                                    &= 300 \\\\
b                                                 &= 300 \times 2 \\\\
b                                                 &= 600 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$600 \ \text{g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-67CE5EA7-B84F-48C9-902F-F787EE74BD8A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A metal rod is $10 \dfrac{4}{5} \ \text{metres}$ long.  How many short rods $\dfrac{3}{10} \ \text{metres}$ 
long can be cut from the longer rod?  

**Show full working**.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Numbers of rods} &= \dfrac {\text {Length of longer rod}} {\text {Length of shorter rod}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \dfrac {4} {5}} {\dfrac {3} {10}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\dfrac {54} {5}} {\dfrac {3} {10}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {54} {5} \times \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {54 \times 10} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times 5 \times 2} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 18 \times \cancel {5} \times 2} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                        &= 18 \times 2 \\\\
                        &= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Numbers of rods} &= \dfrac {\text {Length of longer rod}} {\text {Length of shorter rod}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \dfrac {4} {5}} {\dfrac {3} {10}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\dfrac {54} {5}} {\dfrac {3} {10}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {54} {5} \times \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {54 \times 10} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times 5 \times 2} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 18 \times \cancel {5} \times 2} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                        &= 18 \times 2 \\\\
                        &= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

There is some coloured water in the jar.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


How much **more** water is needed to make $\bold{2 \ \text{litres}}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Water needed} &= 2 - 1.3 \\\\
                     &= 0.7 \ \text{litres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.7 \ \text{litres}$

</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

                     &= 0.7 \ \text{litres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-55EE8A7B-471C-4247-BC0B-6023B61F7656'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

There are $120$ boys and $80$ girls at a small primary school. During the holidays, $50\%$ of the boys and $60\%$ of the girls saw the latest Harry Potter movie. How many pupils in total from the primary school saw the movie ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total pupils who saw the movie}   &= \dfrac {50} {100} \times {120} + \dfrac {60} {100} \times {80} \\\\
                                         &= \dfrac {50 \times 120} {100} + \dfrac {60 \times 80} {100} \\\\
                                         &= \dfrac {5 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} + \dfrac {6 \cancel 0 \times 8 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                         &= 5 \times 12 + 6 \times 8 \\\\
                                         &= 60 + 48 \\\\
                                         &= 128
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$128$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total pupils who saw the movie}   &= \dfrac {50} {100} \times {120} + \dfrac {60} {100} \times {80} \\\\
                                         &= \dfrac {50 \times 120} {100} + \dfrac {60 \times 80} {100} \\\\
                                         &= \dfrac {5 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} + \dfrac {6 \cancel 0 \times 8 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                         &= 5 \times 12 + 6 \times 8 \\\\
                                         &= 60 + 48 \\\\
                                         &= 128
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$128$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $13 + 54 \div 6 - 5 \times 7$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

According to BIDMAS

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 13 + 54 \div 6 - 5 \times 7 \\\\
&= 13 + 9 - 5 \times 7 \\\\
&= 13 + 9 - 35 \\\\
&= 22 - 35 \\\\
&= - 13
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$- 13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>According to BIDMAS
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 13 + 54 \div 6 - 5 \times 7 \\\\
&= 13 + 9 - 5 \times 7 \\\\
&= 13 + 9 - 35 \\\\
&= 22 - 35 \\\\
&= - 13
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$- 13$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

                            &= \dfrac {84 \times 730} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {61320} {100} \\\\
                            &= 613.2
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Subtract three fifths from four sevenths.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {7} - \dfrac {3} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {7 \times 5} - \dfrac {3 \times 7} {5 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {35} - \dfrac {21} {35} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 - 21} {35} \\\\
&= - \dfrac {1} {35}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$- \dfrac {1} {35}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {7} - \dfrac {3} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {7 \times 5} - \dfrac {3 \times 7} {5 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {35} - \dfrac {21} {35} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 - 21} {35} \\\\
&= - \dfrac {1} {35}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$- \dfrac {1} {35}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Calculate two and one third multiplied by one and three sevenths.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \ \dfrac {1} {3} \times 1 \ \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7} {3} \times \dfrac {10} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 10} {3 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times 10} {3 \times \cancel {7}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3 \dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \ \dfrac {1} {3} \times 1 \ \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7} {3} \times \dfrac {10} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 10} {3 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times 10} {3 \times \cancel {7}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3 \dfrac {1} {3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D3368E54-0CBA-4F01-A1ED-0216CE3E57EE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A chocolate bar is shared amongst two friends. 

Alfred first eats one third of the bar. Barbara then eats two thirds of the remaining chocolate.

What fraction of the original chocolate bar is left? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{fraction eaten by alferd}    &= \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= 1 - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {1 \times 3} {1 \times 3} - \dfrac {1}{3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {3} {3} - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {3 - 1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
\text{fraction eaten by barbara}   &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \dfrac{2} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {4} {9} \\\\
                                   &= 1 - \dfrac {4}{9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {1 \times 9} {1 \times 9} - \dfrac {4}{9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9-4} {9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {5} {9}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {5} {9}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{fraction eaten by alferd}    &= \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= 1 - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {1 \times 3} {1 \times 3} - \dfrac {1}{3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {3} {3} - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {3 - 1} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
\text{fraction eaten by barbara}   &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \dfrac{2} {3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 3} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {4} {9} \\\\
                                   &= 1 - \dfrac {4}{9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {1 \times 9} {1 \times 9} - \dfrac {4}{9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9-4} {9} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {5} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {5} {9}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F4ED2C3C-A52E-4DFA-B9E5-EDA177B567AC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In the first week of the screening of a new film a cinema sells $2140$ tickets. 

In the second week it sells $320$ less tickets than the first week.

In the third week it sells $210$ less tickets than the second week.

How many tickets are sold over the three weeks?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question, tickets sold

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total ticket sold }    &= 2140 + (2140 - 320) + (2140 - 320 - 210) \\\\                                                                 
                             &= 2140 + 1820 + 1610 \\\\
                             &= 3960 + 1610 \\\\
                             &= 5570
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5570$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question, tickets sold
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total ticket sold }    &= 2140 + (2140 - 320) + (2140 - 320 - 210) \\\\                                                                 
                             &= 2140 + 1820 + 1610 \\\\
                             &= 3960 + 1610 \\\\
                             &= 5570
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5570$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1A84B2C9-4A73-42D5-8C02-943A1F327DCC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


What fraction of this flag is shaded?How many more rectangles need to be shaded to fill $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of the flag?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of flag shaded}              &= \dfrac {\text{Count of Shaded boxes}} {\text{Total boxes}}  \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {5} {6 \times 5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{5}} {6 \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
\text{Rectangles required}                  &= (\dfrac {2} {3} \times 30) - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {60} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {20 \times 3} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= 20 - 5 \\\\
                                            &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {6}, 15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of flag shaded}              &= \dfrac {\text{Count of Shaded boxes}} {\text{Total boxes}}  \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {5} {6 \times 5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{5}} {6 \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
\text{Rectangles required}                  &= (\dfrac {2} {3} \times 30) - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {60} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {20 \times 3} {3} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} - 5 \\\\
                                            &= 20 - 5 \\\\
                                            &= 15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {6}, 15$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8B2CB059-508A-4AB4-BD70-951C18329CC4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A transport company’s vans each carry a maximum load of $12$ tonnes. A firm needs to deliver $24$ crates each weighing $5\ \text{tonnes}$. How many vans are needed?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total vans needed}                &= \dfrac {\text{Total crates} \times \text{weight of 1 crate}} {\text{Maximum load van can take}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {24 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 12 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{12} \times 5} {\cancel{12}} \\\\
                                        &= 2 \times 5 \\\\
                                        &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total vans needed}                &= \dfrac {\text{Total crates} \times \text{weight of 1 crate}} {\text{Maximum load van can take}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {24 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 12 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{12} \times 5} {\cancel{12}} \\\\
                                        &= 2 \times 5 \\\\
                                        &= 10
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B97B18CD-B34D-453F-BF68-4ECBC5621406'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the sum of the first $40$ whole numbers ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First number}                             &= 0 \\\\
\text{Last number}                              &= 39 \\\\
\text{Total numbers}                            &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the sum of  first 40 whole numbers.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {\text{Total numbers}} {2} \times (\text{First term + Last term}) \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {2} \times (0 + 39) \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 20} {2} \times 39 \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 20} {\cancel{2}} \times 39 \\\\
&= 20 \times 39 \\\\
&= 780
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$780$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First number}                             &= 0 \\\\
\text{Last number}                              &= 39 \\\\
\text{Total numbers}                            &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's find the sum of  first 40 whole numbers.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {\text{Total numbers}} {2} \times (\text{First term + Last term}) \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {2} \times (0 + 39) \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 20} {2} \times 39 \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 20} {\cancel{2}} \times 39 \\\\
&= 20 \times 39 \\\\
&= 780
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$780$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

In a school there are $465$ boys, $531$ girls, and $89$ staff. How many people are there in the school?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total people} &= 465 + 531 + 89 \\\\
&= 1085
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1085$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total people} &= 465 + 531 + 89 \\\\
&= 1085
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1085$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D30B2018-F522-4962-8607-0CC146C6A765'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A train carriage has $62$ seats, if the train has $12$ carriages what is the largest number of passengers it can seat?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest no. of passengers} &= 62 \times 12 \\\\
&= 744
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$744$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest no. of passengers} &= 62 \times 12 \\\\
&= 744
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$744$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5E19CBBE-54A1-4C50-9739-F3060E708FD9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many spots are there on a normal six-sided dice?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total spots} &= 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 6 \\\\
 &= 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

As we know, opposite sides of a dice always add up to 7.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total spots}                                 &= \dfrac {6} {2} \times 7 \\\\
                                                   &= 3 \times 7 \\\\
                                                   &= 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total spots} &= 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 6 \\\\
 &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As we know, opposite sides of a dice always add up to 7.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total spots}                                 &= \dfrac {6} {2} \times 7 \\\\
                                                   &= 3 \times 7 \\\\
                                                   &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$21$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-03309616-DD87-4C53-89CA-064E7763B9C2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The bus from Redhill to London can hold $58$ passengers. If the bus travels to London $18$ times a day, what is the maximum number of passengers that can get to London?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum passengers} &= 18 \times 58 \\\\
&= 1044
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1044$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum passengers} &= 18 \times 58 \\\\
&= 1044
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1044$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

In a school there are $386$ boys, $435$ girls, and $76$ staff. How many people are there in the school?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total people} &= 386 + 435 + 76 \\\\
&= 897
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$897$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total people} &= 386 + 435 + 76 \\\\
&= 897
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

\end{aligned}

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-32DC6924-3D42-4FC1-9F0E-EEE526815D65'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The train from Brighton to London has $12$ carriages and each carriage can hold $48$ passengers. What is the largest number of passengers that can travel on the train? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest no. of passengers} &= 48 \times 12 \\\\
&= 576
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$576$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest no. of passengers} &= 48 \times 12 \\\\
&= 576
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$576$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Martin the builder needs to build a wall $19$ bricks wide and $14$ bricks high. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many bricks will there be in the wall?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bricks} &= 19 \times 14 \\\\
&= 266
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$266$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bricks} &= 19 \times 14 \\\\
&= 266
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$266$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

He can lay 7 bricks every $5 \ \text{minutes}$. How long will it take him to build the wall? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= \dfrac {\text{Total bricks}} {7} \times 5  \\\\
&= \dfrac {266} {7} \times 5 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 38 \times 5} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{7} \times 38 \times 5} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 38 \times 5 \\\\
&= 190 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{190}{60} \\\\
&= \dfrac{19 \times 2 \times 5}{2 \times 5 \times 6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{19 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{5} \times 6} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text{hours} \ 10 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3 \ \text{hours} \ 10 \ \text{minutes or } 190 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} &= \dfrac {\text{Total bricks}} {7} \times 5  \\\\
&= \dfrac {266} {7} \times 5 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 38 \times 5} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{7} \times 38 \times 5} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 38 \times 5 \\\\
&= 190 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{190}{60} \\\\
&= \dfrac{19 \times 2 \times 5}{2 \times 5 \times 6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{19 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{5} \times 6} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text{hours} \ 10 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3 \ \text{hours} \ 10 \ \text{minutes or } 190 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-866159B1-4B81-4AB7-8216-C0F736137635'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The London Eye has $32$ passenger capsules, each of which can take up to $18$ people. What is the maximum number of passengers that can travel at any one time?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum passengers} &= 18 \times 32 \\\\
&= 576
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$576$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum passengers} &= 18 \times 32 \\\\
&= 576
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$576$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6D64BD13-96F2-4FFB-B6AA-B83638EF2A64'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A sunflower is one hundred and fifty centimetres tall.

How tall will it be if its height increases by ten per cent?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

If the height is increased by $10\%$ then new height will be $100 + 10 = 110\%$ of the orginal price.

Using this to get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New Height}  &= 150  + 110 \% \text { of} \ 150 \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {110} {100} \times 150 \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {110 \times 150} {100} \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {11 \cancel {0} \times 15 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                   &= 150 + 11 \times 15 \\\\
                   &= 150 + 165 \\\\
                   &= 315 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$315 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>If the height is increased by $10\%$ then new height will be $100 + 10 = 110\%$ of the orginal price.
<emptyline>
Using this to get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New Height}  &= 150  + 110 \% \text { of} \ 150 \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {110} {100} \times 150 \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {110 \times 150} {100} \\\\
                   &= 150 + \dfrac {11 \cancel {0} \times 15 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                   &= 150 + 11 \times 15 \\\\
                   &= 150 + 165 \\\\
                   &= 315 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$315 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-ABB3027C-A3A6-4680-A41E-B49861EF0A45'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I got $30 \%$ on a $10$-problem test, $70 \%$ on a $20$-problem test and $80 \%$ on a $30$-problem test.

If the three tests are combined into one $60$-problem test, what percentage is my overall score?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Overall score} &= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 10 + \dfrac {70} {100} \times 20 + \dfrac {80} {100} \times 30 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {10 \times 10} {100} + \dfrac {70 \times 20} {100} + \dfrac {80 \times 30} {100} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {100} {100} + \dfrac {14 \times 5 \times 20} {20 \times 5} + \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 6 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                                 &= 1 + 14 + 6 \times 4 \\\\
                                 &= 15 + 24 \\\\ 
                                 &= 39 \\\\
\text {Overall percentage}       &= \dfrac {39} {60} \times 100 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10} {2 \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 13 \times \cancel {2} \times 5 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                                 &= 13 \times 5 \\\\
                                 &= 65 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$65 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                                 &= \dfrac {100} {100} + \dfrac {14 \times 5 \times 20} {20 \times 5} + \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 6 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                                 &= 1 + 14 + 6 \times 4 \\\\
                                 &= 15 + 24 \\\\ 
                                 &= 39 \\\\
\text {Overall percentage}       &= \dfrac {39} {60} \times 100 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10} {2 \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 13 \times \cancel {2} \times 5 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$65 \%$
</code></pre>
